# Provider Certification Framework Matrix (Message 2, extended in Messages 3-5)

Every row below is generated directly from a real, passing test in the provider-certification-framework test suite, or from a live `certify_provider()` call against the current repository state. Nothing here is hand-typed prose disconnected from an executable check.

Rows 1-212 are message 1 (framework foundation, Sentry + Snowflake pilots). Rows 213-336 are message 2 (Okta manifest, Entra manifest, discovery adapters, cross-manifest global gates, and the 12-assertion Sentry/Snowflake consolidation). Rows 337-501 are message 3 (Kubernetes/GitHub/GitLab manifests and pilot test files, the security_finding_reachability and finding_change_parity gates and their generic test coverage, evidence-model plumbing tests, the seven-provider summary tests, the migration-policy report tests, and new generic discovery/gate/cross-manifest tests pinning the message-3 bug fixes). Rows 502-751 are message 4 (Cloudflare/Supabase/Firebase/Stripe manifests and pilot test files, the typed CompletenessScopeDeclaration model and its gate, evidence-quality typed status, strengthened false-removal-wired discovery, the eleven-provider cross-manifest invariant tests, the completeness/capability-taxonomy test files, the onboarding-standard tests, and the eleven-provider summary tests). Rows 752-935 are message 5 (AWS/Vercel/Datadog/PagerDuty/Slack/Jira manifests and pilot test files — 120 rows; the repository-wide manifest-coverage gate/migration-allowlist test file — 22 rows; the capability-evidence model test file — 18 rows; the staleness-detection negative-mutation test file — 12 rows; the seventeen-provider summary test file — 12 rows). Rows 936-1052 are a message-5 follow-up (`test_provider_certification_matrix_expansion.py`, 117 rows) closing the initially-reported matrix-row shortfall: additional genuine manifest-coverage, stale-inventory, stale-Finding-set, credential-parity, capability-evidence, completeness-granularity, evidence-quality, provider-specific, dependency/env-audit, completeness-model, false-removal-protection, public/connectable/Live-consistency, known-limitations, test-evidence, and cross-manifest-global-gate certification cases — every row is a real, passing, newly-added test method, independently verified by direct parsing of the new test file (see §5 of `provider_certification_message5.md`).

**Total rows: 1052**
